03:23:22 --> 03:23:27
I didn't I wasn't sure I would survive

03:23:24 --> 03:23:30
this so it was so hard

03:23:26 --> 03:23:33
and what happened was I was invited to

03:23:30 --> 03:23:36
give a talk about this in New York

03:23:33 --> 03:23:38
it was kind of a spoken word event

03:23:35 --> 03:23:41
uh about science but like personal

03:23:37 --> 03:23:42
experiences related to science this was

03:23:40 --> 03:23:44
almost a year after

03:23:41 --> 03:23:46
my book came out

03:23:43 --> 03:23:48
in my book

03:23:45 --> 03:23:50
one of the first chapters is a chapter

03:23:47 --> 03:23:52
about this experience

03:23:49 --> 03:23:54
but what I realized now is that I wrote

03:23:51 --> 03:23:56
it from the third person perspective

03:23:54 --> 03:23:58
I knew the facts

03:23:55 --> 03:24:00
but I was not emotionally connected to

03:23:57 --> 03:24:02
that experience however

03:23:59 --> 03:24:04
since I wanted to write the book and to

03:24:01 --> 03:24:06
connect to my readers I allowed the boy

03:24:03 --> 03:24:08
to write it

03:24:05 --> 03:24:10
so a lot of people were touched by it

03:24:07 --> 03:24:12
and they would people would say wow that

03:24:09 --> 03:24:14
chapter you know it really got a lot of

03:24:11 --> 03:24:15
resonance it was translated into other

03:24:13 --> 03:24:17
language even before the book was

03:24:14 --> 03:24:19
published

03:24:16 --> 03:24:23
I was surprised by this because I didn't

03:24:18 --> 03:24:26
know yet so the adult Edward was not yet

03:24:22 --> 03:24:28
in touch but the book gave the outlet

03:24:25 --> 03:24:29
o the child

03:24:27 --> 03:24:31
so

03:24:28 --> 03:24:33
and that kind of

03:24:30 --> 03:24:34
started the process

03:24:32 --> 03:24:35
o finally

03:24:33 --> 03:24:39
um

03:24:34 --> 03:24:40
almost a year later I'm in New York in

03:24:38 --> 03:24:43
this

03:24:39 --> 03:24:45
at this event and the night before

03:24:42 --> 03:24:47
I'm in my hotel room and I was like okay

03:24:44 --> 03:24:49
what am I going to talk about tomorrow

03:24:46 --> 03:24:51
and I take a piece of paper it's just

03:24:48 --> 03:24:53
you know my usual like preparation you

03:24:50 --> 03:24:55
know for things and then suddenly I have

03:24:52 --> 03:24:58
this Vision that I will walk up to the

03:24:54 --> 03:25:01
microphone tomorrow and I will just

03:24:57 --> 03:25:03
art crying

03:25:00 --> 03:25:04
and

03:25:02 --> 03:25:06
it's like

03:25:03 --> 03:25:09
but by that time I already had an

03:25:06 --> 03:25:12
Insight that it's possible to have that

03:25:08 --> 03:25:15
kind of a splitting kind of dissociation

03:25:11 --> 03:25:16
I was not but if things were happening

03:25:14 --> 03:25:18
quickly there was someone in my life who

03:25:15 --> 03:25:20
explained to me this idea that

03:25:17 --> 03:25:23
some things are under the radar of

03:25:19 --> 03:25:24
awareness but they may still influence

03:25:22 --> 03:25:27
you

03:25:23 --> 03:25:30
and a lot of that could be connected to

03:25:26 --> 03:25:32
some experiences in your childhood so I

03:25:29 --> 03:25:35
was kind of ready for it from different

03:25:31 --> 03:25:37
angles but I was so surprised because I

03:25:34 --> 03:25:40
was like what is there to remember I

03:25:36 --> 03:25:41
know I know everything so then my inner

03:25:39 --> 03:25:43
voice says all right then you have

03:25:40 --> 03:25:45
nothing to worry about

03:25:42 --> 03:25:48
so we'll go tomorrow

03:25:44 --> 03:25:50
and you will speak about this and if you

03:25:47 --> 03:25:52
start crying it's not a problem I was

03:25:49 --> 03:25:52
like no I don't want to cry in front of

03:25:51 --> 03:25:54
people

03:25:51 --> 03:25:55
I want to find out what it is what

03:25:53 --> 03:25:58
happened

03:25:54 --> 03:26:01
and I sat on my bed closed my head

03:25:57 --> 03:26:02
and it came

03:26:00 --> 03:26:06
so

03:26:01 --> 03:26:09
it's hard to describe so this is what

03:26:05 --> 03:26:11
and the sheer energy of it and how much

03:26:08 --> 03:26:13
effort it took to suppress it actually

03:26:10 --> 03:26:16
for all these years how much effort it

03:26:12 --> 03:26:19
ook to build that

03:26:15 --> 03:26:22
in Russian you know that that hardcore

03:26:18 --> 03:26:24
you know around myself yeah so that and

03:26:21 --> 03:26:27
the thing later I realized there were

03:26:23 --> 03:26:30
moments when it could come out

03:26:26 --> 03:26:32
and for instance I developed this fear

03:26:29 --> 03:26:35
of public speaking and all kinds of

03:26:31 --> 03:26:38
little things that I now feel were

03:26:34 --> 03:26:41
connected so anyway I saw what happened

03:26:37 --> 03:26:44
now Through The Eyes of that child I saw

03:26:40 --> 03:26:47
how difficult it was how crushed he was

03:26:43 --> 03:26:49
and it looked completely hopeless and I

03:26:46 --> 03:26:52
felt like what's the point of living now

03:26:48 --> 03:26:54
for me now that I know how cruel this

03:26:51 --> 03:26:57
world is which I didn't realize before

03:26:53 --> 03:27:01
because I prefer to wear this pink you

03:26:56 --> 03:27:03
know the the rose Rose Colored Glasses

03:27:00 --> 03:27:05
but then something happened it's so

03:27:02 --> 03:27:06
strange

03:27:04 --> 03:27:09
it's like you feel that inside of you

03:27:06 --> 03:27:13
there is this dead child and it is

03:27:08 --> 03:27:15
incredibly sad I mean it's like

03:27:12 --> 03:27:18
I can't even describe it but suddenly he

03:27:14 --> 03:27:21
comes alive and suddenly it's like

03:27:17 --> 03:27:23
oh he's here and I and I got a little

03:27:20 --> 03:27:25
talk with him and I said look you know I

03:27:22 --> 03:27:28
know now and thank you I'm so sorry that

03:27:24 --> 03:27:30
I neglected you for so long you know I

03:27:27 --> 03:27:31
didn't know

03:27:29 --> 03:27:34
thank you

03:27:30 --> 03:27:36
for doing this and it's almost like you

03:27:33 --> 03:27:38
know I felt like the image came to mind

03:27:35 --> 03:27:40
is like a fallen soldier like you leave

03:27:37 --> 03:27:43
a fallen soldier on the battlefield a

03:27:39 --> 03:27:45
wounded soldier and then you come back

03:27:42 --> 03:27:46
to take him with you

03:27:44 --> 03:27:48
and

03:27:45 --> 03:27:51
I said but look look what we have

03:27:47 --> 03:27:53
done look at us now it was not in vain

03:27:50 --> 03:27:55
we are doing okay

03:27:52 --> 03:27:57
and it's kind of almost just like

03:27:54 --> 03:28:00
holding

03:27:56 --> 03:28:03
holding that

03:27:59 --> 03:28:06
child and that

03:28:02 --> 03:28:09
sense of who I am you know and

03:28:05 --> 03:28:10
feeling it so

03:28:08 --> 03:28:12
the next day

03:28:09 --> 03:28:15
I went to the microphone and I let him

03:28:11 --> 03:28:17
speak for the first time

03:28:14 --> 03:28:20
about his experience in his own voice

03:28:16 --> 03:28:22
it was incredible people were crying and

03:28:19 --> 03:28:24
afterwards came up to me and started

03:28:21 --> 03:28:26
sharing stories and so on because it is

03:28:23 --> 03:28:28
a story it's a universal story it's

03:28:25 --> 03:28:31
archetypal story

03:28:27 --> 03:28:33
it's the story of rejection and

03:28:30 --> 03:28:37
being treated unfairly

03:28:32 --> 03:28:40
we all know it and I think it's so

03:28:36 --> 03:28:42
important to realize that

03:28:39 --> 03:28:45
it's so it's possible to revisit those

03:28:41 --> 03:28:46
moments it's possible to reconnect to

03:28:44 --> 03:28:48
ur little ones it's possible to bring

03:28:45 --> 03:28:52
them back

03:28:47 --> 03:28:54
and we are better for it because this

03:28:51 --> 03:28:55
changed my life this experience then

03:28:53 --> 03:28:57
they were then suddenly it's like a

03:28:54 --> 03:28:58
floodgates there were many other things

03:28:56 --> 03:29:01
that came

03:28:57 --> 03:29:03
that's when I became

03:29:00 --> 03:29:05
interested in the dimensions of

03:29:02 --> 03:29:07
imagination and intuition and so on

03:29:04 --> 03:29:09
because suddenly I realized that I was

03:29:06 --> 03:29:11
deprived of those of that possibility of

03:29:08 --> 03:29:13
looking at the World Through The Eyes of

03:29:10 --> 03:29:15
a child because that child was frozen in

03:29:12 --> 03:29:19
time it was I was not connected to him

03:29:15 --> 03:29:22
but suddenly he's with me and he's like

03:29:18 --> 03:29:27
almost like opens his doors and says

03:29:21 --> 03:29:27
look good look at this look at this

03:29:28 --> 03:29:34
o

03:29:31 --> 03:29:37
if I could ask you

03:29:33 --> 03:29:40
about there's a difficult idea here

03:29:36 --> 03:29:42
there's a tension I've interacted with a

03:29:39 --> 03:29:45
few folks in my personal life and in

03:29:41 --> 03:29:48
general that have lived through uh this

03:29:44 --> 03:29:50
experience of unfairness and cruelty in

03:29:47 --> 03:29:52
the world as young

03:29:49 --> 03:29:55
people

03:29:51 --> 03:29:55
and

03:29:54 --> 03:30:02
what wisdom do you draw from

03:29:57 --> 03:30:04
the the the the action you took of not

03:30:01 --> 03:30:06
acknowledging that you're a victim to

03:30:03 --> 03:30:09
Cruelty but instead just working your

03:30:05 --> 03:30:13
ass off working harder

03:30:08 --> 03:30:15
and then this the flip side of that is

03:30:12 --> 03:30:17
you eventually reconnecting with the

03:30:14 --> 03:30:20
cruelty that you experience because if

03:30:16 --> 03:30:22
you did that early on I was not ready

03:30:19 --> 03:30:24
for it it is a defense mechanism I could

03:30:21 --> 03:30:25
be I could have come you know there were

03:30:23 --> 03:30:27
kids who commit suicide after this

03:30:24 --> 03:30:29
experience I could have commit suicide

03:30:26 --> 03:30:31
because it's too much

03:30:28 --> 03:30:33
and it is well known afterwards of

03:30:30 --> 03:30:36
course I became aware of all the

03:30:32 --> 03:30:39
literature about childhood trauma and so

03:30:35 --> 03:30:42
n and I have been I've been speaking

03:30:38 --> 03:30:45
publicly about it since then too and so

03:30:41 --> 03:30:47
you know it is well-known issue and

03:30:44 --> 03:30:47
well-known

03:30:46 --> 03:30:51
um

03:30:46 --> 03:30:54
kind of a universal phenomenon I think

03:30:50 --> 03:30:56
that interesting enough

03:30:53 --> 03:30:58
even though one now I see a lot of

03:30:55 --> 03:31:01
discussion of it now that my eyes are

03:30:57 --> 03:31:03
open but somehow before I didn't see it

03:31:00 --> 03:31:06
so that which also shows you how our

03:31:02 --> 03:31:08
confirmation bias kind of like how we

03:31:05 --> 03:31:09
screen ourselves how we turn the Blind

03:31:07 --> 03:31:11
Eye to things which do not confirm our

03:31:08 --> 03:31:13
views or for which we are not yet ready

03:31:10 --> 03:31:16
and by the way nobody should push to do

03:31:12 --> 03:31:19
it too soon I'm glad I I developed

03:31:15 --> 03:31:23
certain force a certain strengths

03:31:18 --> 03:31:26
I was confident

03:31:22 --> 03:31:29
um I was strong to withstand this and if

03:31:25 --> 03:31:31
I weren't who knows how it could turn

03:31:28 --> 03:31:32
out so it is a very subtle kind of

03:31:30 --> 03:31:34
alchemical process

03:31:31 --> 03:31:37
which I don't think there is a recipe

03:31:33 --> 03:31:39
there's a formula

03:31:36 --> 03:31:41
the reason I'm talking about this is

03:31:38 --> 03:31:43
just to share this experience because I

03:31:40 --> 03:31:45
think that the only thing we can do in

03:31:42 --> 03:31:49
this in some sense is to share with each

03:31:44 --> 03:31:51
other because then we can find

03:31:48 --> 03:31:53
for instance if somebody

03:31:50 --> 03:31:55
shared with me

03:31:52 --> 03:31:57
it would naturally Lead Me Maybe to get

03:31:54 --> 03:32:00
closer to that to kind of understanding

03:31:56 --> 03:32:02
it's really just personal stories it's

03:31:59 --> 03:32:05
not

03:32:01 --> 03:32:07
obviously there is a there is a

03:32:04 --> 03:32:09
component where professionals could be

03:32:06 --> 03:32:11
involved professional therapists and so

03:32:08 --> 03:32:14
n in my case it somehow happened

03:32:10 --> 03:32:16
miraculously well I did have support but

03:32:13 --> 03:32:18
not not from professional therapists but

03:32:15 --> 03:32:20
from like dear friends

03:32:17 --> 03:32:22
o I did I did have you didn't you do

03:32:19 --> 03:32:24
need I had somebody at the time who

03:32:21 --> 03:32:26
basically held my hand and through this

03:32:23 --> 03:32:28
experience yes it was invaluable and it

03:32:25 --> 03:32:33
could not be done otherwise

03:32:27 --> 03:32:35
so I think it's very common and here's

03:32:32 --> 03:32:35
the thing

03:32:35 --> 03:32:43
I would not do it in any other way

03:32:39 --> 03:32:46
when you when I reconnected

03:32:42 --> 03:32:48
and I saw all the horrors and so on but

03:32:45 --> 03:32:51
I also was able to see

03:32:47 --> 03:32:53
that my examiners were victims

03:32:50 --> 03:32:56
of Their Own

03:32:52 --> 03:32:58
situation that they became they fell for

03:32:55 --> 03:33:01
this bogus

03:32:57 --> 03:33:04
eries or maybe it was more of a issue

03:33:00 --> 03:33:06
of career advancement or something and

03:33:03 --> 03:33:08

03:33:05 --> 03:33:10
as well because they must have had some

03:33:07 --> 03:33:13
kind of consciousness of conscience

03:33:09 --> 03:33:14
about it that acting this way towards

03:33:12 --> 03:33:16
ort of

03:33:13 --> 03:33:18
basically kids you know

03:33:15 --> 03:33:21
so it's not it wasn't pretty from their

03:33:17 --> 03:33:25
point of view so I could forgive them

03:33:20 --> 03:33:27
and I could like also appreciate what a

03:33:24 --> 03:33:30
boost of energy it gave me yeah

03:33:26 --> 03:33:33
if I was accepted and I was just where I

03:33:29 --> 03:33:35
was a first-year student I lived I would

03:33:32 --> 03:33:36
live in a dorm because I was in the you

03:33:34 --> 03:33:38
know I'd be probably partying and

03:33:36 --> 03:33:40
rinking and who knows what maybe I

03:33:37 --> 03:33:42
wouldn't even become a mathematician

03:33:39 --> 03:33:45
but this

03:33:41 --> 03:33:47
focused me like a laser without me even

03:33:44 --> 03:33:49
thinking about it it just happened I

03:33:46 --> 03:33:51
just I couldn't do I didn't care about

03:33:48 --> 03:33:53
anything but doing mathematics and it

03:33:50 --> 03:33:56
paid off you know it changed my life so

03:33:52 --> 03:33:58
was it good or bad

03:33:55 --> 03:34:01
Paradox

03:33:57 --> 03:34:04
seems like life is full of those

um you said you lost your father four

03:34:03 --> 03:34:08
years ago yeah

03:34:04 --> 03:34:10
what have you learned about life from

03:34:07 --> 03:34:13
your dad that's another another big one

03:34:09 --> 03:34:17
yeah because I was very close to him

03:34:12 --> 03:34:20
and uh it was tough it was tough

03:34:16 --> 03:34:23
and I was not

03:34:19 --> 03:34:25
I was sort of not ready for it because

03:34:22 --> 03:34:27
up until that point I lived pretending

03:34:24 --> 03:34:30
that death does not exist

03:34:26 --> 03:34:32
when my grandparents died I was already

03:34:29 --> 03:34:34
in the U.S

03:34:31 --> 03:34:37
so it's very convenient and I couldn't

03:34:33 --> 03:34:39
go back so I grieved but it kind of was

03:34:36 --> 03:34:42
a bit abstract for me I didn't see their

03:34:39 --> 03:34:47
dead bodies you know I didn't

03:34:41 --> 03:34:49
bury them and so on so I waited till so

03:34:46 --> 03:34:50
my the first death in my life was my

03:34:48 --> 03:34:53
father

03:34:49 --> 03:34:55
like of a really close loved ones and I

03:34:52 --> 03:34:59
was absolutely devastated

03:34:54 --> 03:35:00
you know he was such an amazing creature

03:34:58 --> 03:35:04
such an amazing human being he was the

03:34:59 --> 03:35:07
kindest the smartest the most funny just

03:35:03 --> 03:35:09
it's really funny and just really fun to

03:35:06 --> 03:35:11
be with you know this is what I miss

03:35:08 --> 03:35:13
obviously I mean I would just love to

03:35:10 --> 03:35:14
hang out with him

03:35:12 --> 03:35:16
so

03:35:13 --> 03:35:19
and then suddenly it's not there

03:35:15 --> 03:35:21
so it's tough but it kind of changed my

03:35:18 --> 03:35:24
perspective you miss him

03:35:20 --> 03:35:27
I miss him tremendously

03:35:23 --> 03:35:29
I miss intermittently but I in a way I

03:35:26 --> 03:35:32
learned that he never left me in some I

03:35:28 --> 03:35:35
mean it sounds so words are so you know

03:35:31 --> 03:35:38
like they are

03:35:34 --> 03:35:39
they cannot express in words this what

03:35:37 --> 03:35:41
I'm trying to say

03:35:38 --> 03:35:43
but

03:35:40 --> 03:35:46
um do you carry him

03:35:42 --> 03:35:48
with you yeah and in some sense I always

03:35:45 --> 03:35:49
did and and I

03:35:48 --> 03:35:56
saw that that it's always been

03:35:52 --> 03:35:58
um it was really we were one in some

03:35:55 --> 03:36:01
sense you know like we were but there

03:35:57 --> 03:36:01
was this experience of

03:36:00 --> 03:36:06
two two people

03:36:02 --> 03:36:08
being together and that I missed

03:36:05 --> 03:36:12
tremendously

03:36:07 --> 03:36:14
but he gave me so much and I you know

03:36:11 --> 03:36:15
let me tell you one aspect of it for

instance

03:36:14 --> 03:36:20
when he was a kid his father was sent to

Gulag on bogus pretenses right so he

when he was 16 he replied to University

he wanted to become a theoretical

03:36:23 --> 03:36:28
physicist

03:36:25 --> 03:36:29
by the way my love for trichophysics was

to the large extent

03:36:28 --> 03:36:33
because of that

03:36:30 --> 03:36:35
and he was not accepted even though he

was brilliant because he was the son of

03:36:34 --> 03:36:39
the enemy of the people

03:36:36 --> 03:36:42
and he

03:36:39 --> 03:36:43
kind of broke him this experience that

03:36:41 --> 03:36:46
he

03:36:42 --> 03:36:47
didn't care when he was you know he went

03:36:45 --> 03:36:50
o technical school and he didn't really

03:36:46 --> 03:36:52
care that's my take on it

03:36:49 --> 03:36:54
and then he ended up in this in this in

this little provincial town and he

and I fell in love

03:36:59 --> 03:37:06

product of that you know of physics but

03:37:05 --> 03:37:10
hen

03:37:06 --> 03:37:12
what I learned is that

03:37:09 --> 03:37:14
because he was not able to overcome that

03:37:11 --> 03:37:17
specific experience

03:37:13 --> 03:37:19
it felt to me to do it and if I didn't

my son of my daughter would have I think

that that was one of the things I

03:37:20 --> 03:37:24
learned

03:37:21 --> 03:37:26
that was not by chance that our about

he same age

03:37:25 --> 03:37:30

ubjected to the same kind of unfairness

and cruelty

it for him also I always because he was

also always so proud of me I was so

happy and I was I had this tremendous

gift

wice I was invited by American

it could be anywhere in the U.S both
